Only relevant for self delivery orders. | AG=Tax on delivery fee. Occurs when cost of delivery is greater than the delivery fee. (where applicable) | AW=Total Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AX=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AY=Provincial Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AZ=Quebec Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BA=Retail Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant (applicable in Manitoba) | BB=Additional consideration Uber charges Merchant equal to the amount of Markup. | BC=Applicable GST/HST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BD=Applicable QST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BE=Applicable PST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BF=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant  | BG=Quebec Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BH=Provincial Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant | BI=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant  | BJ=Quebec Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BK=Provincial Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant | BL=Where delivery fee is greater than cost of delivery, this is equal to (delivery fee minus the cost of delivery) where applicable | BM=For merchants who perform their own delivery, the tip from the Eater to the courier | BN=Description of adhoc one-time payments or charges from Uber to the merchant | BO=All miscellaneous payments like Ad payments, Ad credits etc. inclusive of tax | BP=Sales tax Uber Eats is required to pay in jurisdictions with marketplace facilitator laws  | BQ=Total payout associated with this order (negative if associated with a refund)  Sales + Adjustments - promotions + marketing adjustment + Bag fee + delivery fee (self delivery) + tips (self-delivery) - marketplace fee +/- Other payments - marketplace facilitator tax  (incl tax where applicable) | BR=Date payout initiated by Uber (generally the Monday following the prior week) | BS=Either: Paid (payment has been sent to bank), Pending (payment has been created in our system), Rejected by Bank (the receiving bank account has not accepted the payment) | BT=Link to the 'Uber to Restaurant' invoice .pdf | BU=Link to the 'Courier to Restaurant' invoice .pdf | BV=Link to the 'Restaurant to Eater' invoice .pdf | BW=This is the ID from retailers, used to identify retailer loyalty customers | BX=The reference ID for identifying the payout related to this order in the Payout Summary Report CSV

Provenance et contrôle des pièces

Les originaux, les déclarations et les analyses sont identifiés par leur provenance. Les doublons et les versions d’une facture ne s’additionnent pas. Une extraction OCR reste à contrôler sur la pièce originale. Les documents à obtenir et les vérifications ouvertes figurent dans les synthèses de chaque dossier.

Les pièces d’origine restent dans leurs dossiers. L’index est une copie de travail datée; une recherche sans résultat ne démontre pas l’absence d’une pièce. Les synchronisations OneDrive et GitHub sont distinctes.
